- **`qt6ct.conf` Home Manager file not yet activated on laptop** (session 35) — **gaming is done** (confirmed live through session 48's many activations); laptop still needs `nh os boot` + reboot.
- **DMS's `qtThemingEnabled` toggle must be flipped by hand on any fresh niri install** (session 35) — intentionally left unmanaged (same reasoning as `settings.json` generally). On a from-scratch install or a wiped `~/.config`, Qt apps will stay light-themed until this is toggled once via DMS Settings → Theme & Colors → "Apply Qt Themes" (or `qtThemingEnabled: true` edited directly in `~/.config/DankMaterialShell/settings.json`), then a theme-mode toggle (`dms ipc call theme light`/`dark`) to force DMS to re-apply.
- **Niri `config.kdl` Home Manager symlink not yet activated on laptop** (session 34) — **gaming is done**; `nh os boot` + reboot on laptop is required before the dotfiles-managed keybinds/gaps/waybar-removal take effect there.
- ~~USB drives not auto-mounting under Thunar on gaming — needs a session restart, not a rebuild~~ (session 48) — **RESOLVED (session 67):** today's reboot gave `dbus-broker.service` a fresh start (`ActiveEnterTimestamp` matches boot time); `gio mount -l` now correctly lists both drives via `GProxyVolumeMonitorUDisks2`.
- **`systemd.services.apparmor.reloadIfChanged = false` workaround in `security.nix`** (session 48) — real nixpkgs packaging bug in `apparmor-parser-5.0.0`: `apparmor-utils`' `aa-remove-unknown` (the service's `ExecReload`) hardcodes a path to `apparmor-parser`'s `lib/apparmor/rc.apparmor.functions`, which this build doesn't ship, so any activation needing to reload AppArmor policies failed outright ("Failed to reload apparmor.service"), blocking `nh os boot` entirely. AppArmor enforcement itself is unaffected. **On each future nixpkgs bump, check whether `apparmor-parser` ships that file again** (`find /nix/store/*apparmor-parser*/lib/apparmor/rc.apparmor.functions`) and remove the workaround once it's fixed upstream — same removal-condition pattern as the electron insecure-package waiver above.
- **DMS may silently fail to auto-inject future `include` lines into `config.kdl`** (session 34) — only `dms/outputs.kdl` is currently included; other DMS-generated files (e.g. `colors.kdl`) are written expecting DMS to add their own `include` line when a matching settings-UI toggle is enabled. Since `config.kdl` is now a read-only Nix symlink, that auto-injection would fail quietly. If a future DMS integration toggle doesn't seem to take effect, check for this and add the `include` line by hand to `dotfiles/common/configs/niri-config.kdl`.
- ~~TEMPORARY `permittedInsecurePackages` waiver for `pnpm-10.29.2` in `nix.nix`~~ (session 29) — **RESOLVED (session 43):** the pending nixpkgs bump fixed vesktop upstream (now builds against generic `pnpm_10`); waiver removed in the same commit that added the electron waiver below. Do not re-add.
- ~~TEMPORARY `permittedInsecurePackages` waiver for `electron-40.10.5` in `nix.nix`~~ (session 43) — **RESOLVED (2026-07-24, commit `839cbc0`):** nixpkgs now ships vesktop on `electron_43` with no known vulnerabilities; waiver removed. Do not re-add.
- ~~`nixpkgs` temporarily pinned back to `567a49d1` (zen kernel breakage)~~ — **RESOLVED (session 29):** upstream ships `bzImage` again in linux-zen 7.1.2; pin lifted by the 2026-07-02 `/flake-update-verify` run (`48c11d3`), verified by building the kernel from cache and checking `bzImage` exists.
- **Avahi mDNS `openFirewall = true` in printing.nix** — `modules/printing.nix:12` exposes UDP 5353 on all interfaces (not just the LAN interface). Closing it would break mDNS-based printer discovery. Proper fix: interface-scoped firewall rules (e.g., `networking.firewall.interfaces.<lan-if>.allowedUDPPorts = [ 5353 ]`). Deferred from 2026-06-03 security audit.
- **`wg0 mtu = 1380` pending on desktop clients** — Committed in shared `vpn.nix` (`eb69c43`) but only applies on each client's next `rebuild`. laptop is currently back at the default 1420 (the live `ip link` fix was lost on wg toggle) — works for normal use, but large transfers through the tunnel can truncate until rebuilt.
